====================00_opening.rpy====================
*I stretch my arms out wide as I take my first step off the bus.*
*My body is stiff after being seated for what feels like half the day, and various joints crack in response to my movement.*
*At the same time I fill my lungs with fresh air. After being trapped inside of a bus all day, the air outside has a refreshing taste to it.*
Iris: "Mmmm... **crack** Ouch!"
Iris: "Aw, geez. It's only been a few hours. Toughen up, Iris!"
*With a loud crack of my neck I finish limbering up and hit the road.*
*I keep an eye out for any street signs that might help me to gather my bearings.*
*While I have a rough idea of where I am, I've never been to this city before, nor am I traveling with anybody who might.*
*I'm all alone in a brand new city; my home for at least the next year.*
Iris: "*sigh...* This is hopeless. I can't see anything helpful."
Iris: "Maybe I should ask for help after all..."
*As if on cue, my cell phone begins to vibrate with an incoming call. It's Dad.*
*After a few rings, I pick up. His voice is cheerful and vibrant.*
Dad: "Iris! I thought it was about time for me to call."
Dad: "How is it? Do you like your new home? Did you have any trouble getting there? It's not too far from the school, is it?"
Iris: "Whoa, whoa, whoa. Slow down, dad. I haven't even seen the place yet."
Iris: "I just got off the bus a minute ago."
Dad: "Oh... Right."
Dad: "Hahaha, whoops. I didn't mean to get ahead of myself."
*My father laughs heartily as he speaks.*
Dad: "So, how was the ride over there? Full of fun and excitement?"
Iris: "Ugh, don't ask."
Iris: "Do you know how many creeps catch the bus? Not to mention the stale air and uncomfortable seats."
Iris: "Worse, I couldn't even afford to doze off. Missing my stop would just be the disaster to top off this momentous occasion."
Dad: "Wow, someone's feeling gloomy."
Dad: "Suck it up, princess. A little bus ride never hurt anyone."
Dad: "And what happened to your enthusiasm from this morning? You were practically jumping for joy at the notion of moving out and attending school."
Dad: "You even changed into your uniform before leaving. You were prancing around like it was your first day of school."
Iris: "I-I was not! I just wanted to make sure it fit!"
Dad: "Yet you're still wearing it now, aren't you?"
Iris: "......"
*I look down at my own body.*
*My legs are clad in knee-socks up to my thighs. They stop a couple of inches before reaching my short skirt, which I have mixed feelings about wearing.*
*The long-sleeved blazer I'm wearing is warm, and around my left breast is the school emblem. Below the blazer is a white shirt, also decorated with the school emblem.*
*Finally, the collar of the white shirt is held in place by a tie, neatly wrapping the whole outfit together.*
Dad: "Ahh, my precious daughter is finally moving out on her own."
Dad: "It feels like just yesterday that I was changing your diaper and reading you bedtime stories."
Dad: "Are you really going to be alright without me?"
Iris: "Oh, please. I'm a grown woman now, dad. I can take care of myself."
Iris: "I'm not so sure about *you*, though. Make sure you eat properly and go to bed at a reasonable time, okay?"
Dad: "Yeeees~"
*I smirk to myself at my father's childish reaction.*
*Although he's a good, responsible man, my father is not a serious person by nature.*
*It's only when something big is going on that he seems to pull his act together.*
Dad: "Well, I won't keep you any longer. Call me when you arrive at your new home, okay?"
Dad: "I'll be around if you need--"
Iris: "A-Ah, wait a second!"
Iris: "Actually, there is one thing I'd like your help with..."
*I take a look around and detail my surroundings to my father.*
Dad: "Hmm... Sounds like you got off one stop too early."
Dad: "Don't worry, your new school isn't far away. Just keep heading towards the heart of the city and you'll find it in no time."
Dad: "And if you still can't find it, Papa will track your phone's GPS and give you street by street directions. How does that sound?"
Iris: "Creepy beyond compare."
Iris: "Thanks for the help, though. I should be able to figure it out from here."
Dad: "Alright. Don't hesitate to call if you get lost again."
Dad: "I remember this one time when we went to the zoo, and I found you--"
*beeeeep*
*I hurriedly press the end call button.*
Iris: "Honestly. Every chance he gets..."
*I put my phone away and start walking.*
*Following my father's directions, I soon find myself in front of my new high school.*
*Lillium High School... A prestigious school inhabited primarily by the children of upper-middle class families.*
*Thanks to my exemplary performance and attitude, not to mention my participation in extra-curricular activities, I was given the opportunity to apply for a scholarship here.*
*Not only was my application successful, but I am now a top student at Lillium High.*
*My battle hasn't ended yet, however. As a scholarship student I must continue to excel, lest my scholarship opportunity be revoked.*
*Furthermore, I will be living by myself from now on, and I don't know anybody in this city.*
*This is my first step towards adulthood, and as nervous as this whole situation makes me, I am without a doubt excited.*
*screech*
*Pulling me away from my thoughts, I turn my head back as the sound of a bus coming to a halt reaches my ears.*
*As the doors open, only a single person steps off.*
*It's a girl carrying a large bag on her back.*
*Even from behind, her long black hair and pure white skin catch my eye.*
*She exudes an unmistakable air of maturity despite appearing to be around my age. A cool beauty with an appeal I find my eyes immediately drawn to.*
*But beyond her appearance, what I find most captivating is her demeanor.*
*She takes confident strides, not at all unbalanced by the large bag she's carrying. She appears strong, yet her arms and legs are not muscular.*
*Her figure is lean, and her back is straight. She pushes her chest out naturally and walks with excellent posture.*
*My age or not, she is undoubtedly more mature and adult than I am.*
*As I stare at her figure, my mouth agape, I notice just how loudly my heart is beating.*
*The mere sight of this girl causes my body to malfunction. My throat is paralyzed, my face is hot, and my heart is beating so quickly I fear it may burst.*
???: "...Yes?"
Iris: "Eep!?"
*My heart jumps as the beautiful young woman addresses me.*
*She stares straight into my eyes, exuding confidence and strength I could only dream of possessing.*
???: "If you don't want anything, then..."
*The young woman turns around and begins to walk off.*
Iris: "A-Ah, no! Please wait!"
???: "Hm?"
*My body reacts before my brain is able to formulate a plan.*
*I don't mean to call out to the girl, but before I know it, I'm unconsciously reaching forward.*
Iris: "I-I... uhm..."
???: "Yeah?"
*The girl raises an eyebrow at my sudden outburst.*
*I think about what I can say, and why I called out to this total stranger. Perhaps I can ask if she's also a student?*
*As I mentally assess my options, I become painfully aware of the girl's increasing annoyance.*
Iris: "No, it's nothing. Sorry."
???: "...Hmph."
*Without saying a word, the young woman turns around once again, this time walking away for real.*
*Simply watching her beautiful figure recede from my sight causes my chest to tighten in a manner I've never experienced before.*
*Once she's gone, my chest suddenly relaxes, and I'm able to breathe once more.*
*But before I can think too deeply about this unfamiliar feeling, our meeting comes to an end.*
*Just who... was that girl?*
*Following a lengthy walk, mostly thanks to my unfamiliarity with the area, I finally arrive at my new home.*
*It's an apartment in the city, not too far away from my new school.*
*Most of the students at Lillium High use the student dorms, but I unfortunately missed out on any vacancies this year.*
*So for the time being, at least until a dorm room opens up, I will be living in an apartment rented by my father.*
Iris: "Ah... Come to think of it..."
*I pull my cell phone out once more and call my father.*
Iris: "Hey, it's me again."
Dad: "Hi "me again", I'm Dad."
Iris: "......"
Iris: "I'm hanging up."
Dad: "A-Ah, hold on! Don't go!"
*I sigh and put my ear up to the phone once again.*
Iris: "Anyway... I was just calling to say that I've arrived at the apartment."
Dad: "Hmm... It took you this long to get there?"
Dad: "You really should exercise more."
*beeeeep*
*I glare at the phone as I hang up.*
Iris: "Honestly, why do I even bother?"
Iris: "I thought moving out would mean being free of my father's nonsense."
*My phone begins to vibrate just as I go to put it down.*
*I sigh once more and answer the call.*
Iris: "Dad, I've only just arrived. If you want to pester me, can't it wait until--"
Dad: "Hold on, hold on. There's something important I need to tell you."
Iris: "Don't go out after 9PM?"
Dad: "No... Well, yes, but something else."
Dad: "I was planning on telling you before you left, but you just looked so cute dancing around in your new uniform that I--"
Iris: "Out with it, Dad."
Dad: "...Okay, okay."
Dad: "The thing is... you won't be living alone after all."
Iris: "...Eh?"
*I stare at my phone in surprise, completely baffled by my father's revelation.*
Iris: "Wh-What do you mean I won't be living alone!?"
Iris: "You promised that you would trust me to take care of myself!"
Iris: "Besides, you work too far away. You can't seriously be thinking of staying with me!"
*My father doesn't respond immediately.*
*I imagine that he is wearing the same expression that I was only moments ago.*
Dad: "...Iris, I think you're misunderstanding something here."
Dad: "What I'm trying to tell you is... you'll be sharing the apartment with another Lillium High School student."
*My eyes slowly open wider as my father's words sink in.*
*I had anticipated a quiet life of solitude during my stay here. A life of school, studying, and not much else.*
*Having a housemate would undeniably change that.*
Iris: "A... Another high school student?"
Dad: "Sorry. I know you were looking forward to living alone."
Iris: "N-No, that's okay!"
Iris: "I'm just surprised. I don't actually think that having a housemate would be a bad thing."
Dad: "Ahahaha... I'm glad to hear that. I'd be in a difficult situation if you refused to live with her."
Dad: "Oh, and don't worry about her having an impact on your studies. Zoey usually keeps to herself."
Iris: "Zoey, huh...?"
Iris: "Okay. So a girl called Zoey will be living with me and attending the same school as I am. I can work with that."
Iris: "Is there anything else I should know before we meet?"
Dad: "Oh... I suppose there is one thing."
*I can hear the sound of my father scratching his face as he hesitates to answer.*
Dad: "You see, you and Zoey already, uhm... have a connection."
Iris: "...A connection?"
*I think back over the people I've met.*
*While I can't recall the names of everyone I've been introduced to, I'm fairly certain I don't know anyone called Zoey.*
Dad: "Yes. Although you've never met, the two of you already share a close bond."
Iris: "Uhh... Okay."
Iris: "So what is this connection or bond, or whatever?"
*My father once again begins to scratch his face.*
Dad: "Iris, Zoey is... How do I put this?"
Dad: "She's... "
Iris: "My little what?"
*I respond in a flat tone, half-convinced that my father is just screwing with me.*
Dad: "Mmm... I guess I should explain."
Dad: "Zoey has lived overseas for most of her life with her mother, so the two of you never had the chance to meet."
Dad: "Which might be for the best, now that I think about it. Zoey can be a bit..."
*My father trails off, unsure of how to best end such a leading statement.*
Dad: "Well, at any rate, a fresh start might be just what each of you need."
Dad: "Okay, talk to you later, honey. Lots of love. Bye, bye, bye."
*beeeeep*
Iris: "...Eh?"
*I stare at the phone in my hand.*
*The call ends before I can ask a single question of my father, who is no longer on the line.*
*All I can hear is the sound of my own breathing.*
Iris: "A... little sister?"
Iris: "You've gotta be kidding me."
*I lived my entire life thinking and being told that I was an only child.*
*Furthermore, my father raised me alone, and never really told me about my mother.*
*I grew up thinking my mother was dead, or perhaps a deadbeat who ran out on us.*
*In reality, it appears that she was living overseas with a little sister I never knew I had.*
Iris: "Unbelievable... I've had a little sister all this time and never even knew it."
Iris: "Now, out of nowhere, we're going to be living together?"
Iris: "This is just too much..."
====================01_first meeting.rpy====================
*Following the piece of news my father had left me at the last minute, I was left in a state of shock.*
*In a daze, I decide to take a quick look around the apartment.*
*I locate the bathroom, my bedroom, confirm the state of my belongings, and finally sit back and take it all in.*
*As hard as it is to believe, this is my new home. An apartment rented by my father, but still mine.*
*But before I can settle down and relax, there is another matter weighing on my mind.*
*The other piece of news my father dropped on me at the last minute. The unplanned guest in my otherwise empty apartment.*
Iris: "A little sister... *My* little sister..."
*For the next thirty minutes I pace back and forth.*
*Over and over again I pass by the boxes containing my belongings. I then enter every room in the house, one by one, before returning to the kitchen.*
*I repeat this process as time slowly passes me by, as though waiting for something, or trying to reach a conclusion.*
*In reality, however, I have no clear goal. There is only a single thought on my mind, and pacing will not make it go away.*
*As one might expect, from the moment my father nonchalantly made his declaration, my thoughts belong to a single person:*
*My little sister, Zoey.*
*Though surprised, after a while I'm not actually disappointed. And I'm certainly not angry.*
*Instead, all I can think about is how I'll finally have the younger sibling I always wanted.*
Iris: "M-Maybe I should lay down for a bit. I really do need to rest."
*Contrary to the advice I offer myself, I continue to pace.*
*The excitement of having a little sister, one with whom I will soon be spending every day, is just too much to keep bottled up inside.*
Iris: "Eheheh~ A little sister... My very own little sister!"
*I begin to imagine what she might look like, and what we could do with one another.*
*Will we cook together? Walk to school together? Will she need my help with her school work?*
*I'm definitely going to be a reliable older sister. I'll make certain that she knows I can be counted on.*
Iris: "Come to think of it, how old is Zoey?"
Iris: "My father said she was younger than me, but he didn't exactly say--"
*ding dong*
*My excited yet nervous pacing comes to a halt as I hear a sound at the door.*
*I'm not expecting any visitors, and Zoey is the only roommate I've been told about.*
*In my mind, the one ringing my doorbell can only be a single person.*
*I ready myself to give her the best greeting that I can.*
Iris: "Zoey!"
*I rush to the front door and throw it open without pause or hesitation.*
*The moment I do, I see a familiar face staring back at me, blankly.*
Iris: "Z... Zoey?"
Zoey: "That's my name."
Zoey: "You're Iris, right?"
Iris: "Y-Yeah... That's right."
*I shrink back and lower my voice.*
*In front of me is the beautiful young woman whom I met earlier today.*
*The cool beauty whom I thought to be my age, if not older.*
*My little sister, Zoey.*
Zoey: "Cool. Looks like I got the right place."
*Zoey pushes past me without another word.*
*She drops her large bag on the floor and places her hands on her lower back, stretching after carrying it around for so long.*
Zoey: "It's just us here?"
Iris: "Eh?"
Iris: "O-Oh, yeah... Uhm, yes. It's just us."
Zoey: "Awesome. Lock the door, will you? I wanna take a look at my room."
Iris: "Oh... um..."
*Zoey opens her bag and takes out a change of clothes.*
*She then takes a quick look around, searching for the bedroom.*
Zoey: "I do have my own room, right?"
Iris: "......"
Zoey: "Oi, I'm talking to you. Do I have a room or not?"
*I look down at my feet as I brace myself.*
*I've spent the past half an hour thinking about this moment, wondering what I would do, what I would say.*
Iris: "Yes, it's down the hall, on the right..."
*Zoey doesn't give a response, instead picking up her bags once more.*
*So before Zoey can end our first meeting as sisters, I force myself to speak.*
Iris: "Zoey, d-do you... uhm..."
Zoey: "Mm?"
Iris: "Do you... want to... "
Zoey: "Haa? Where the hell did that come from?"
Iris: "I... I just, Uh..."
Zoey: "Look, I don't know what our father told you, but I'm just here to eat and sleep, got it?"
Zoey: "So go make yourself useful and unpack, or something. I'll find the bedroom myself."
*Zoey heads down the hallway and checks a few rooms.*
*She soon finds her room and enters, closing the door behind her.*
Iris: "......"
*Even after Zoey has left my sight, I continue to look towards the bedroom.*
Iris: "Agh... *smoothies*? What the hell?"
*Standing in the hallway, I continue to replay that unbelievably awkward exchange in my head.*
*What just happened? The girl from the park...that was Zoey?*
*My little sister, whose existence I only just learned about, pushed straight past me, without any indication whatsoever that she even wanted to know me.*
*Furthermore, Zoey is younger than me, and yet... she's so tall and looks so mature for her age.*
*In a way, it almost feels like I'm the one who should be *her* little sister.*
*Suffice to say, this is definitely not how I pictured my first day of independence.*
Iris: "......"
*I feel so weird just leaving our first encounter off on that note...*
*What should I do?*
*No... I shouldn't take it personally.*
*She's probably just really tired from traveling. She did come from out of town, so she probably just needs some alone time to rest.*
*I shouldn't judge Zoey's personality off of a few short interactions. I'll probably have a chance to spend more time with her tomorrow.*
*With that, I begin preparations for sleep.*
====================02_first day of class.rpy====================
**beep**beep**beep**
*Groggily, I wake up the next morning when my alarm goes off at 7:00.*
*I hit the snooze button several times and doze until finally hauling myself out of bed.*
Iris: "Ugh... *yaaawn*"
*I go to the bathroom to get washed up, and notice that one of the towels hanging there is already wet. Apparently Zoey already got her morning shower.*
*By the time I emerge emerge from the bathroom, Zoey is nowhere to be seen in the apartment. *
Iris: "Zoey...?"
*No answer.*
Iris: "....."
Iris: "After what happened yesterday, I'm not sure whether to feel disappointed or relieved."
*As I expected, homeroom on the first day is introductions and going over the school's conduct handbook.*
*After homeroom, my next class is calculus. It's right next door, and I'm one of the first students to arrive. I take a seat near the door.*
Iris: "Hi! Nice to meet you again."
Classmate: "Hi, Iris!"
*The students trickle in one by one, and I say hello to the classmates I already know, while offering a friendly nod to the new faces.*
*Right as I'm about to take a seat, a familiar face pops in.*
*My eyes widen as I realize it's Zoey's.*
*She pokes her head around the corner of the door frame, as if unsure as to whether she's found the correct room, then steps through after confirming the number.*
Iris: "Hi, Zoey!"
Zoey: "Huh?"
Iris: "I'm so glad you came by to see me!"
Zoey: "What are you talking about...?"
Iris: "That...that is why you're here, right? To say hello to your sister?"
Zoey: "....."
Iris: " I don't mean to hold you up, you should get to your next class."
Zoey: "This is my next class."
Iris: "You're taking calculus? But I thought you were a second-year student?"
Zoey: "This is the class that the assessment test placed me into."
Iris: "Wow, really?"
*Zoey shrugs and walks into the room, scanning for an open seat. I pull out the chair next to me.*
Iris: "You can sit here!"
*Instead of sitting in the seat I pulled out, Zoey walks right past me and takes a seat at the other end of the room.*
*She looks directly away from me, towards the windows.*
Loud Classmate: "Wow, did Iris just get stood up?"
Haughty Classmate: "How embarrassing! Though, she looks like a really plain girl...if I were that girl, I might sit somewhere else, too."
Loud Classmate: "Haha, that's so mean!"
Haughty Classmate: "But it is true..."
*I hear the murmurs of students around me reacting to my interaction with Zoey, and blood rushes to my face. Looking down shamefully, I clench my hands in my lap.*
*Uu... Why did I have to let my guard down like that?*
*I'm thankful when the teacher finally calls the class to order.*
Teacher: "Quiet, everyone."
Teacher: "Now, I have no intention of allowing the group classroom structure to devolve into social hour. We'll be having assigned seating. "
*The classroom's collective groan hits my ears.*
Teacher: "Everyone, get up and find your new seats."
*I look at the seating chart and suddenly realize that the new arrangement has put my seat right next to Zoey's. I locate my seat, and Zoey plops herself down next to me.*
*Ugh, whose idea of a cruel joke is this...?*
*No, I can't be like that. Let's make an effort, at least...*
*I turn my head to smile at Zoey, but she avoids eye contact with me, instead looking towards the front of the room with a perpetually bored expression on her face.*
*What should I do now?*
*I try to focus on the teacher's lesson, but I can't stop thinking about Zoey.*
*She's sitting right next to me, yet she seems completely oblivious to my existence.*
*And not only do I have to spend the rest of the semester sitting next to her in calculus class, but we're living together in the same apartment?*
*What have I gotten myself into?*
====================03_hallway confrontation.rpy====================
*After a long day at school, I gradually make my way home.*
*In both hands I'm carrying plastic bags filled with groceries, not to mention the school bag on my back.*
Iris: "I'm baaaack..."
*I call out, as I enter the apartment.*
*The bags in either hand are placed on the counter, followed by my school bag.*
*I then collapse on the nearest chair and close my eyes, exhausted from my trip.*
*Normally, after the first day of school, I'm filled with excitement.*
*But all I can feel now is the crushing anxiety of what will happen the next time I see Zoey.*
*It seemed like she outright hated me in math class. There's no other way to interpret it, right?*
*I sigh and close my eyes.*
Zoey: "Damn... Where did I put it?"
Zoey: "Hrm..."
Iris: "...Hm?"
*My eyes shoot open as I hear a familiar voice.*
*I had assumed Zoey wasn't home, or perhaps otherwise engaged, since my initial greeting didn't receive a response.*
Iris: "Zoey? You're home?"
Iris: "Why didn't you respond when I called out?"
Zoey: "I'm on my way out, anyway."
Zoey: "Don't pay me any mind. I'll be out of your hair soon."
*True to her words, Zoey appears to be getting ready to leave.*
*Even though I only just returned myself, it's getting dark, so I can't see why she would choose to go out now of all times.*
*I pull myself onto my feet and face Zoey.*
Iris: "You're going out now?"
Zoey: "That's what I said."
Iris: "But... isn't it getting a bit late for that?"
Iris: "It'll be dark soon. You don't want to be wandering around an unfamiliar city at night."
Zoey: "You mean that *you* don't want to wander the city. Don't assume to know what I want."
Iris: "Well... Okay, that's fair."
Iris: "But why would you go out at this time? I already bought some groceries to use for dinner, if that's what you were worried about."
Zoey: "I'm not going shopping."
Iris: "Then where are you going?"
Zoey: "Give me a break."
*After that, she begins moving towards the door again.*
Iris: "Wait!"
*I take a step to the side so that I block her escape. She raises her eyes mutinously. It takes me a several seconds to get my voice to work.*
Iris: "You know, seeing as we both are up..."
*I nearly cringe as I hear my own nervous voice.*
Iris: "Why don't we study?"
*Zoey opens her mouth to reply, then closes it again. For one shocking moment, I think she's actually going to consider my proposal.*
*Then I realize she's only taking a deep breath to keep her temper in check.*
Zoey: "No."
*Zoey begins to brush past me in the hallway. I can't just let her leave!*
Iris: "Y-You can't!"
Zoey: "Can't what?"
Iris: "Go outside right now! It's night time!"
Zoey: "So...?"
Iris: "Our school enforces a curfew. Any student found out past 10 o'clock is going to be in serious trouble! Even...expulsion."
*At the mention of school, Zoey's eyes narrow.*
Iris: "A-And safety, too!"
Iris: "Just because we live on our own, doesn't mean we can do whatever we want."
*bang*
Iris: "Eep!?"
*Zoey grabs me by the shoulders and pushes me against the wall.*
*She's forceful, and puts great strength into her hands.*
*I'm not injured, but my heart is sent racing, both due to Zoey's proximity and the sudden shock of being thrown into something solid.*
Iris: "Z-Zoey...? What are you..."
Zoey: "Shut up."
Zoey: "What I choose to do is my business, and nobody else's."
Zoey: "You have no right to tell me what to do."
*Zoey's tone is cold and fierce.*
*Her fingertips are sinking deep into my flesh, and I start to feel pain in my shoulders.*
Iris: "Zoey... I'm not trying to be the bad guy here."
Iris: "I just don't want you to get yourself in trouble."
Zoey: "If my actions get me in trouble, then that's my problem, not yours."
Iris: "B-But I'm your sister! I don't want to see you get hurt or anything..."
*Zoey clenches her teeth.*
Zoey: "Don't give me that shit."
Zoey: "You think a day of knowing one another and some vague blood relation makes us sisters? Don't make me laugh."
Zoey: "You're just a nosy little do-gooder who can't keep out of other peoples' business."
Iris: "N-No, I..."
Iris: "I'm just trying to look out for you..."
Zoey: "By telling me how to live my life?"
Zoey: "Listen closely; I'm not going to be bossed around by some pampered little girl who thinks she's better than me."
Zoey: "Take your ego and shove it. I want nothing to do with your holier than thou bullshit."
*I bite my lip as I force back tears.*
Iris: "I... I don't think I'm better than you! Why would you say that!?"
Iris: "From the moment I first saw you, I thought you were an amazing person! A cool, independent woman who can take care of herself."
Iris: "Not only that, but you're attending Lillium High. Even though you're younger than me, you made it into this prestigiouss school."
Iris: "I'm not looking down on you in the slightest, Zoey. And I know this might sound strange, since I'm supposed to be your older sister, but..."
Iris: "From the moment I laid eyes on you, I thought you were beautiful beyond compare!"
Zoey: "......"
*What... What the hell am I saying?*
*Zoey releases my shoulders and takes a step back.*
*Her face is contorted in frustration and confusion as she tries to process what I've just told her.*
*But rather than respond, Zoey turns around and heads towards the door.*
Zoey: "Just... Just stay out of my way, alright?"
Zoey: "I won't warn you again."
*Zoey slams the door shut behind her.*
*I stand there stunned before I breathe again, realizing I'm alone.*
Iris: "Uu..."
Iris: "Why did that..."
*I just got told off by my little sister, and on top of that, she seems to hate me even more now, too!*
*I wring my hands in front of me, still unmoving from where Zoey backed me against the wall.*
Iris: "What am I going to do...?"
====================04_second day of class.rpy====================
*The next morning, I wake up sore, slumped over the desk in my room.*
Iris: "Ugh..."
*My bedroom light is still on, and I'm still in my clothes from yesterday.*
*Oh... That's right. I'd stayed up last night to try and catch Zoey when she came back home, but I must have fallen asleep before she got back.*
*I quickly change out of my grungy clothes and grab a granola bar from the kitchen on my way out the door. No time for breakfast today.*
*As I hurry to school, thoughts of last night rush through my head.*
*Inside, I dread seeing Zoey during second period calculus. Having to sit next to her all class, after what happened last night...*
*On the bright side, maybe this will be a chance for me to offer her a proper apology.*
*She can't escape me when it comes to class, right?*
*After the bell rings to signal the end of homeroom, I rush to calculus. I want to be here when Zoey arrives. *
*I watch as the students file in, one by one.*
Iris: "......"
*As I wait for Zoey, gradually the other seats around me fill, until finally the teacher calls the class to order. Zoey's seat remains conspicuously empty.*
Teacher: "Alright, class. Settle down. I'm going to pass around the attendance sheet, so be sure to sign it before you leave."
*The teacher begins his lesson, and I find myself continually checking the door, hoping to see Zoey stepping through.*
*Several minutes pass, and I still see no sign of her.*
*Eventually, the attendance sheet is passed to me. I locate my own name on the sheet and mark it off.*
*I hesitate before passing the sheet on.*
*I can see Zoey's name listed below on the paper...*
*The rest of the day passes uneventfully.*
*After my last class, I check around the sophomore classrooms to see if I can find Zoey.*
Iris: "I don't see her anywhere. Maybe she went straight home?"
*There isn't any warm welcome after I rush back to the apartment at record speed. *
*Not that I was expecting one, but Zoey is nowhere to be found.*
*I try knocking on Zoey's bedroom door.*
*No response.*
*My phone rings. I look down at the screen and realize it's from my dad.*
Iris: "Dad?"
Dad: "Iris? Where is Zoey?"
Iris: "I...don't know."
Dad: "I called her phone and she's not picking up. Can you go home to see if she's there?"
Iris: "I'm home. I just checked her door, but she's not responding. She might still be at school."
*I can hear the sound of Dad sighing on the other end of the line.*
Dad: "I just got a call from the school. Zoey didn't go to class today."
Dad: "I thought this would be different. With her at a new school, and living with you..."
Iris: "What do you mean?"
Dad: "This isn't the first time this has happened, you know. Zoey had these same problems at her old school."
Dad: "Iris, I'm disappointed. I wanted you to be a positive example for Zoey, not encourage this kind of behavior."
Dad: "I know she's not going to start acting like an angel overnight, but the least you could do is try to keep her in line!"
Iris: "I-I'm sorry, Dad. I'll try to keep an eye on her."
Dad: "I know you're not the one who's the problem. It's not fair to take this all out on you when Zoey's the one who's cutting class. I just...I hope things improve, Iris."
Iris: "Me too, Dad. I hope things get better soon."
Dad: "Well, I guess that's all. I don't want to get another call like that from the school."
Iris: "Goodbye, Da-"
*He hangs up, cutting me off in mid-sentence.*
Iris: "......"
*I don't think I've ever heard Dad so angry. At least, he's never been that angry at *me*...*
Iris: "Why is it *my* fault? Zoey misbehaves, and I'm the one who gets scolded?"
*I didn't even know I had a sister before now, and now I’m responsible for this unruly person?*
Iris: "No, that's not right... Zoey is still younger than me. If I want to be an older sister, I do need to set a good example for her."
====================05_snooping on zoey.rpy====================
Iris: "But how am I even supposed to keep an eye on her? It's not like she tells me where she goes."
*Unless...*
*My eyes glance towards the narrow apartment hallway that contains our bedrooms.*
Iris: "I could look in her room..."
*I'm the only one home at the moment, and it's not like it would be locked.*
Iris: "No, no. It's wrong to do that. I can't."
Iris: "..."
*But... it might give some clue to where she's been going during the day. What if she's doing something really shady?*
*In spite of my misgivings, I soon find myself standing in front of Zoey's bedroom door.*
*I place my hand on the doorknob, hesitating.*
Iris: "I really shouldn't do this. Really..."
*But... after what happened yesterday, I think Zoey has lost the right to complain about me being rude. Not showing up to school, and then letting me get the flack for her cutting class...*
*I twist the knob with a feeling of unease, and push the door open.*
*Doesn’t look like she decorated at all in here. Even the blankets are so plain looking.*
Iris: "Hmm..."
*I'm not sure when Zoey will be back, so it's best to do this quickly.*
Iris: "I don't see anything in here..."
Iris: "I feel really bad now. Why am I even doing this..."
*As I turn to leave, something on the table catches my eye.*
*Colorful papers are sticking out from underneath Zoey's textbooks on the table. Is it a class handout?*
Iris: "....."
*I've already come this far... May as well.*
Iris: "!!"
*I pick up one of the sheets. It's a glossy flier. "Dora's Tap. Monday night specials! Live music show, featuring local talent!"*
*I glance over the other papers. *
Iris: "Club Life... Whiskey Row... Entropy Club..."
Iris: "What...? Why does she have fliers for bars?"
*What the heck is this? It looks like she has a lot of them, too! And one of them is even circled with marker around the date!*
*My head is spinning. Is this what Zoey has been doing the last few nights? Going out to bars?*
**click**
*Horrified, I spin to face the door. It's opening! Zoey is back!*
*Roughly, I shove the fliers under the books.*
*When I straighten, I find a Zoey staring at me, her eyes wide.*
*The shocked expression doesn't last long, however, as her eyes narrow to critical levels.*
Zoey: "What are you doing in my room?"
Iris: "In your room?"
*I echo her, stupidly. *
Iris: "Oh... right. I was, um, I was going to see if... If you wanted to watch TV?"
*If it was even possible, Zoey's eyes somehow narrow further.*
Zoey: "Why?"
*I knot my hand in the bottom hem of my sweater, trying to keep my voice from sounding like a tightrope. *
Iris: "Um... Something good might be on?"
*Zoey turns me around first, then presses her palms flat against my back. She pushes me from her room as I weakly protest. *
Iris: "We don't even have to watch TV. We could study or something. I mean -"
Zoey: "Good *night*, Iris."
*She practically hisses at me as she roughly closes her door in my face. I jump at the sound of it slamming.*
Iris: "Phew, that was too close for comfort."
*But those fliers... I definitely saw something weird in there.*
Iris: "Zoey isn't a bad girl... is she?"
====================06_tailing zoey.rpy====================
*The next morning, Zoey isn't any friendlier.*
*I mean, that's normal, we didn't talk much before. But this time, the silence is heavy.*
*Whenever I so much as move a finger, Zoey's eyes cut towards me with a distrustful and menacing glare. It's excruciatingly awkward to eat my breakfast.*
*Zoey's pushes her plate away when she finishes her meal.*
Iris: "Oh, I can clean that up for you-"
Zoey: "If you think you can make me forget about you snooping through my room by being "nice", forget it."
*I flinch at her biting remark. Urgh. I really wish I hadn't looked in her room now. I can't possibly ask her about the fliers, either.*
*Zoey turns her nose up at me, getting out of her seat and swiftly depositing the plate in the sink.*
Iris: "Are you leaving...?"
Zoey: "......"
*With a final parting glare, Zoey leaves through the front door.*
*The cool surface of the dining room table collides with my head as I drop onto it. I've really made a mess of things.*
*After waiting a good three minutes so that Zoey can't glare at me for following her, I finally leave the apartment and make my way to school.*
*I'm more than a little anxious as calculus approaches.*
*Sitting through another round of sharp glares from my younger sister isn't exactly on top of my list of fun.*
*But the situation resolves itself, because...*
Teacher: "Zoey White? Miss White?"
Teacher: "She's absent again...?"
*The teacher twists his mouth disapprovingly and shoots me a look, before going back to attendance. *
*I sink down into my chair.*
*Why am *I* getting blamed for her ditching class?*
Iris: "Though... even I’m starting to get worried."
*Once school is over, I head out of the front entrance.*
*I'm about to head home, when I see a familiar tall figure walking away from the school-gate.*
Iris: "Zo--"
*My mouth clamps shut as she rapidly heads out of view.*
*What the...?*
*Zoey isn't going the way home. In fact, she's walking the complete opposite direction.*
Iris: "Why is she going that way?"
*And what's more, if she was at school, why wasn't she in calculus...?*
Iris: "......"
Iris: "This is my chance to find out where she goes all day..."
*I don't want to assume the worst about Zoey, but why on earth would she have those fliers in her room?*
*She isn't meeting people at those bars, right? She isn't meeting... men?*
Iris: "....."
*After a quick mental battle, my feet take off after Zoey.*
Iris: "Phew. Following people isn't easy."
*I've almost lost sight of her how many times now? And just by walking through the park. If I didn't know any better, I’d say she was intentionally trying to make following her impossible.*
*!!*
*Zoey zips down a path to the right, breaking out into a dash. I round the corner after her-*
Iris: "Ack!"
*My foot gets caught on a stray branch in the path. Grumpily, I pull a twig out of my sock.*
Iris: "This is not my forte."
*Quietly, I sneak over to the path again. It's now devoid of anyone.*
Iris: "Oh no! I lost her!"
*Did she see me following her? How could she not? Argh. *
*Besides, I’m being totally unfair. This could all be a huge coincidence. *
Iris: "!! Ahh!"
*Out of reflex, I spin behind a tree.*
*Zoey has just stepped out of a nearby bathroom, now fully changed into her casual clothing. She's also carrying a huge black bag over her shoulder.*
Iris: "What's going on? She changed her clothes in a park bathroom?"
*Zoey is moving swiftly through the park now, and making her way for the exit. She's going towards the station. *
Iris: "....."
*My fallback plan was to claim I was enjoying a stroll in the park if she saw me, but if she's getting on the subway...*
*...there's no way I can pretend that I’m not following her.*
Iris: "......"
Iris: "I can't turn back now."
Iris: "I have to see what she's up to."
Iris: "Uu..."
Iris: "I was barely able to get off at the same stop as Zoey, and now I’ve lost her altogether!"
*I nervously glance at my surroundings, at the strange place Zoey had got off the subway for.*
Iris: "Uu... this place is really scary."
*There's nothing but bars around here. I can't even imagine what this place looks like later at night. *
*Standing around in my school uniform, I really stick out like a sore thumb. I guess I should feel lucky it's not busy.*
Iris: "But where did Zoey go?"
*Wait... the name of the flier that was circled... was that tonight? "Entropy Club". *
*Which, to my luck, happens to be right in front of me.*
Iris: "....."
*I step towards the Entropy Club. The barred doors and blackened windows don't allow a single glimpse of what's inside.*
*My only indication that it's actually open for business is the music and smell.*
*There's something so off-limits about this place... This entire street, really. Something that screams "you don't belong here".*
Iris: "I must be insane! I've followed my sister, snooped in her room, and now I’m about to go into a seedy bar looking for her??"
Iris: "But I've come too far to just give up. I'll duck in, look for Zoey, and then get out before anyone says anything..."
Iris: "Ugh, now I’m muttering to myself in this dingy place. Let's get this over with."
*The smell of acrid cigarette smoke overwhelms my senses as soon as I enter the bar.*
*I resist the urge to pull my shirt up over my nose. *
*It's so dark in here that my eyes have to adjust before I can begin to find my way around. *
*Everyone is facing away from the door, watching the stage. The bar is in a location further in, so I’m able to slip in unnoticed.*
*I hug the back wall, scanning the bar for signs of Zoey, when I see-*
Iris: "N-No way!"
*Is that... Zoey?*
*My eyes go wide as I see her up on the stage, guitar in hand.*
*She's softly singing into the microphone and playing while doing so.*
Iris: "No way...."
*Rooted in place, I quietly listen as her song washes over the bar.*
*I can't believe this beautiful song is coming from the same girl who seems to have a permanent scowl on her face.*
Iris: "......"
*Zoey's smooth fingers glide along the guitar strings, playing it with a clearly practiced hand. *
*Her voice is so warm, so soothing... That the bar and its few inhabitants fade around me.*
*The lyrics ease along in tandem with the strumming of her guitar.*
*Her harsh clothing is such a contrast to the gentleness of the song, that in a strange way, it almost seems to make it sweeter.*
*I continue to listen, enraptured, as the girl I learned was my little sister only a few days ago captivates the small audience with her singing.*
*Until all too soon, the song tapers off. My surroundings rebound to reality as Zoey preps for the next song.*
Iris: "So Zoey comes to these places to sing..."
Iris: "How could I be so stupid?"
*I jumped to so many conclusions about Zoey, and none of them were good.*
*Guilt stabs into me. I timidly remove my eyes from the girl on stage. *
Iris: "I'm the worst. No wonder she doesn't like me..."
Man: "Hey, little lady..."
Iris: "H-Huh?"
*I look up, now aware that someone is addressing me.*
*A man is next to me by the wall, leaning in towards me. How long has he been there?*
Man: "You're a little young to be in this place... Y'know, the uniform. Or is that your kink? Heh..."
*Euch... His breath stinks of alcohol. I hold back a gag as he advances closer, now leaning on the wall with his arm.*
Man: "Can I get you a drink? If you're not legal, I don't mind buyin', hehe."
Iris: "N-No thank you."
*The man leans in even more, and I take a step back.*
Iris: "!"
*I’m trapped between him and the corner between the wall and a large table. My heart starts to pound in my chest.*
Iris: "....."
Man: "Geez, relax. I'm obviously trying to do something nice for ya'."
*I flinch as the man's voice raises. My eyes dart around for an exit - He's got me pinned back here!*
Iris: "Please leave me alone."
Man: "Chill out. I'm just offering you a drink!"
*The man sneers at me, but doesn't move away. In fact, he's moving even closer!*
Man: "You're the one dressed like *that* in a bar, what d'you think guys are gonna do? Huh?"
Iris: "Uu..."
*On the verge of tears, I look down at my feet. I hear the man chuckle.*
Man: "Come on, just one drink..."
*The man stumbles, and harshly grabs my arm as he almost trips.*
Iris: "Ah!"
*I shut my eyes tight as I almost fall.*
*The man's grip is like a vice on my wrist as he tries to steady himself.*
*Why did I come here? Why did I follow Zoey to this place?*
*Is this what I deserve? For thinking the worst of Zoey?*
*Please just leave me alone!*
Zoey: "{size=+8}Get the hell away from her!{/size}"
*My eyes shoot open as a voice shouts through the suddenly very quiet bar. It's then that I finally register the lack of music.*
*Up on the stage, Zoey is now standing up, looking right at us. Her eyes are dangerously focused, and her chest heaves in and out at a rapid pace.*
Iris: "Zoey?"
Man: "Waah?"
Zoey: "I said get away from her!"
Man: "Whaat? What the hell... I was just talking to 'er!"
*Zoey unhooks her guitar from the amp, resulting in an ear stinging pitch throughout the bar. *
Zoey: "I'm warning you!"
*Zoey grips her guitar like a bat as she hops off the stage, quickly advancing towards us.*
*Now every eye in the bar is turned towards us, watching the spectacle.*
*The man stumbles back as Zoey is almost on us.*
Man: "Whatever. Crazy bitch..."
*The man retreats, mumbling obscenities and throwing his arms into the air.*
Manager: "What in blazes are you doing?!"
*A short, red-faced man in a vest storms over. He's glaring daggers at Zoey, who stares back.*
Manager: "Getting off stage and harassing my customers?! Are you mental!?"
Manager: "I took a leap of faith letting you in here. I want you gone! Now!"
Zoey: "Tch. Whatever. This place blows."
Zoey: "Let's go, Iris."
*Not daring to speak a word, I follow Zoey out of the bar. The squat man voices his outrage at us the entire trek out.*
====================07_after tailing.rpy====================
*Once we're a short ways from the bar, Zoey stops, turning to face me.*
*My heart is pounding so fast from the encounter, that I'm starting to shake a little. *
Zoey: "Are you okay?"
Iris: "I-I'm fine. I'm just... not used to that."
Zoey: "It's probably a good thing that you're not."
Iris: "......"
Zoey: "Now then..."
Zoey: "Why are you following me, Iris?"
*Urk. Zoey puts a hand on her hip, twisting her mouth into a scowl. I look away, not able to meet her accusatory gaze.*
Iris: "I was really worried..."
Iris: "You've been missing classes and... I wasn't sure if you'd gotten mixed up into something dangerous or not."
Iris: "But I'm the one who is in the wrong... I made some really bad assumptions about you."
Zoey: "......"
Iris: "I'm really sorry."
*I hang my head. I don't know what else to say. Zoey probably really hates me now, and with good reason.*
Zoey: "... I'm sorry I worried you."
Iris: "H-Huh? Y-You are?"
Zoey: "I guess it did look suspicious coming out here."
*That's an understatement, but I’m not going to push it. Zoey shrugs.*
Zoey: "Truth be told, I'm not even totally sure what I'm doing."
Iris: "You're not?"
*Zoey shakes her head, and then looks around.*
Zoey: "Listen, how about we get out of this place first? "
*I nod in agreement.*
*I lag behind Zoey as we walk, my eyes downcast.*
*Zoey had changed back into her uniform in the park bathroom, stating the smell of the cigarette smoke sticking to to her other clothes was making her feel sick.*
*I can't help but let out a heavy sigh. The days events really wore me out.*
*I guess that's to be expected. It's been a roller coaster of emotions all day.*
Zoey: "......."
Zoey: "Here, you should sit down."
*Zoey leads me to a bench. I'm not physically tired, but I comply. Sitting on a bench doesn't sound so bad.*
*To my surprise, Zoey sits down next to me, too.*
Iris: "I'm sorry I got you in trouble with that guy..."
Zoey: "You didn't get me in trouble."
Iris: "If I hadn't been there, that guy wouldn't have made you leave..."
Zoey: "The manager? That guy was a creep, anyways."
*We sit in silence for a while while I collect my thoughts.*
*Weirdly, the one on the forefront of my mind isn't having to prowl through strange bars, or that weird guy.*
*But instead the amazing feeling of seeing Zoey up on stage.*
Zoey: "W-Why are you smiling like that? You're weirding me out..."
Iris: "I'm just so happy!"
Iris: "I finally get to sit and talk to you like this, and on top of that, I found out you're an amazing singer!"
Zoey: "What? I'm not. I-I only sing because I couldn't get a gig by just doing guitar."
Iris: "I was really surprised. Your voice is so beautiful."
Zoey: "S-Stop complimenting me. It's embarrassing."
*Zoey rubs the back of her neck, staring off into the park. She seems like she's going to say something, but then she stops, furrowing her brows.*
*Then she moves to rise off the bench. Instinctively, I grab her wrist.*
Iris: "Can we... stay like this a little while longer?"
Iris: "If we go back to the apartment, you're not going to hate me again, are you?"
Zoey: "Look, I... "
Zoey: "I don't... hate you..."
*Zoey stiffly crosses her arms, giving me a weak excuse. Despite trying my best not to, I feel a scowl creeping up on my face.*
Zoey: "I don't. I just..."
Zoey: "When I first learned about you and your life, you seemed like some totally pampered girl. "
Iris: "That's-"
Zoey: "It's not your fault. I feel stupid for even being mad now."
Zoey: "I just..."
*Zoey trails off, looking anywhere but in my general direction.*
*After a long silence, it seems Zoey isn't going to say anything more. I decide to assure her.*
Iris: "Well now we can put it all behind us!"
Iris: "But, you know, you really shouldn't be going to places like... that."
Zoey: "I need to make money."
Iris: "M-Money? What for?"
*Zoey gestures to her guitar, which is leaning against the bench in the grass.*
Zoey: "I left all my equipment at school when I... came here. I need money to buy good stuff, but more importantly, I'll get rusty if I don't play."
Zoey: "I thought if I did a few free shows, the owner might overlook my age and pay me. But obviously, that's not gonna happen."
Iris: "I'm sorry..."
Zoey: "Don't be. It's not a huge loss."
Iris: "But... playing in *bars*?"
Zoey: "I thought it would be a good idea to play and make some money."
Iris: "You could have just told me."
Zoey: "Would you really have accepted that I wanted to play in bars for money?"
Zoey: "I didn't want to risk you crying to dad."
Iris: "I would have accepted that you wanted to play, Zoey. There's got to be other ways to do it."
Zoey: "Your school doesn't have a music department for anything that's not classical. I also don't want to disturb people in our apartment by practicing there, either."
Iris: "But there's got to be a better way than this!"
Zoey: "If I wanted to, I could fall in with some kids who are just doing it as a hobby, but that doesn't really solve my money problem."
Zoey: "And I have nowhere to earn legitimately because I'm not old enough. I just blew my shot with the one guy seedy enough to let me play."
Zoey: "I'll have to waste the next two years just doing nothing."
*Zoey was so stressed out that she'd go to these lengths...*
*Making up my mind, I turn to face Zoey.*
Iris: "Zoey, I’ll help you!"
Zoey: "You will?"
Iris: "Of course! What are sisters for, after all?"
*Zoey stares at me, at a loss for words.*
*I can't help but chuckle at how silly she's being... Of course I'll help her.*
*We can start up a club at school! Maybe I can join too, as the manager or something. It'll be so much fun-*
Zoey: "You'll let me use your name on applications?!"
Iris: "....."
Iris: "Huh?"
Zoey: "Oh, thank you, thank you! Iris. I'm so sorry I judged you..."
*Before I can protest, Zoey gives me the most radiant smile I've ever seen. Then, if it couldn't get any more amazing, Zoey wraps her arms around me in a hug. *
Iris: "Applications...?"
Zoey: "You can only play in most of these places if you're old enough. With your name, I’ll be able to pass the background screening no problem."
Iris: "U-Um..."
Zoey: "I might even be able to get in with a band... That would be some seriously good luck."
*Zoey mutters her future plans as I wait for my head to stop spinning from the contact of her warm, gentle hug.*
*Ugh. Am I really this starved for affection? *
*And more importantly, what did I just agree to...?*
====================08_ice cream date.rpy====================
*I pack my books and stationery as the final bell rings.*
*School is over at last.*
*I rise to my feet and head towards the door, hoping to catch Zoey before she leaves.*
*Just as I begin to walk away from my desk, however, someone blocks my path.*
Professor: "Iris, do you have a minute?"
Iris: "Oh... Of course."
*I walk to the side and allow my other classmates to pass.*
*Once it's just my teacher and I, he begins to talk.*
Professor: "Iris, I wanted to talk to you about your score on the Chemistry test from the other day."
Iris: "**gulp** Y-You do?"
Professor: "I do. And going by the look on your face, I'd say you know exactly what I'm about to say."
*I grimace as I think about how badly I must have performed on that test.*
*Between moving out and moving in with my suddenly announced younger sister, my mind was anywhere *but* on the exam.*
Professor: "Iris, need I remind you are one of the top candidates for Lillium's prestigious scholarship?"
Professor: "You can't afford to let your marks fall. Your eligibility is entirely dependent on your grades. "
Iris: "I... I realize that, I really do. But..."
Professor: "But what? You have more important things to do?"
Professor: "This is your future we're talking about. Whatever is going on in your life, you can't afford to throw away this opportunity."
Professor: "If you continue to do poorly, you *will* have your scholarship revoked. Don't forget that."
*Before I can respond, my teacher walks off, leaving me alone to reflect on what he said.*
*So I really did that badly, huh? I was worried that my home situation might affect my grades, but I didn't think it would be that bad...*
*I need to do something about this.*
*I grab my belongings and turn to head towards the door once more.*
Zoey: "What a jerk. I'm glad he doesn't teach my class."
Iris: "No, he's not a..."
Iris: "Whoa! Zoey? When did you get here?"
Zoey: "About the time that good-for-nothing started laying into you."
Zoey: "Who does he think he is, anyway?"
Zoey: "So your marks slipped a little. That's no reason to get bent out of shape."
Iris: "No... It might be hard to hear, but he was right."
Iris: "I am eligible for a scholarship that this school provides. If I don't keep my grades perfect, it could go to someone else."
Iris: "He just cares about my future, that's all."
Zoey: "If you say so..."
Iris: "Anyway, forget about that. Were you waiting for me?"
Zoey: "Waiting for you?"
Iris: "Well, you usually go right home after school, right?"
Iris: "Could it be that you actually wanted to walk your big sister home?"
Zoey: "Hmph. Don't get too full of yourself. And quit with the "big sister" thing."
Zoey: "I just happened to be passing by when I saw you being lectured. That's all."
Iris: "Oh... I see."
*I let out a deep sigh.*
Iris: "I guess you're right. I shouldn't get so full of myself."
Iris: "My grades are dropping... my home life has warped beyond my control... and my own sister hates me."
Iris: "I have no reason to feel proud of myself."
*My shoulders droop more and more as I speak.*
*While I don't truly believe what I just said, I don't think it's too far off the mark, either.*
*This could very well be an important crossroads in my life.*
Zoey: "Oh, come on. You don't have it that bad."
Zoey: "It was only one test, wasn't it? And it's not like I really... *hate* you, or anything."
*I shake my head.*
Iris: "It's okay, Zoey. You don't need to comfort me."
Iris: "What I should be doing right now is studying, not fishing for sympathy."
Iris: "I'm going to head home. I'll understand if you want to go a different way than me."
*I push past Zoey and head towards the door once more.*
*Once we leave the school, Zoey and I continue to walk together. Or as close to together as the two of us seem to get.*
*Zoey walks a few steps behind me, far enough away that we could be strangers, but not so far that we might get separated.*
*When I stop, she stops, and when I continue, so does she.*
*What is Zoey today? A lost puppy, or a stalker?*
*I slow to a halt.*
Iris: "Say, Zoey..."
Zoey: "Yes?"
Iris: "How long are you going to keep on following me like this?"
Zoey: "I'm not following you. We do happen to live in the same building, remember?"
Iris: "Oh..."
*I start walking once again.*
*Once we reach an intersection, I begin to take a left turn, where I should have taken a right.*
Zoey: "You still don't remember the way back? You were supposed to go right."
Iris: "I'm not going straight home, okay?"
Zoey: "I see..."
*I keep walking the path away from our home, leaving Zoey at the intersection alone.*
*At first she just stands there, watching me walk away.*
*After a few seconds, however, I hear her footsteps creeping up behind me.*
Iris: "I thought you said you weren't following me?"
Zoey: "I'm not. I just happen to be making a detour on my way home too."
Iris: "Hmph. Whatever you say."
Iris: "Well, I'm going this way, so--"
Zoey: "I like smoothies."
Iris: "Huh?"
Zoey: "Smoothies. I like them."
*...Ooookayyy...*
Zoey: "There's a place around here that sells them, right?"
Zoey: "Will you take me?"
*I turn around to face Zoey.*
*As always, she looks dead serious. There isn't an ounce of joy or compassion in her voice or on her face.*
*Despite that, I can't help but think back to our first meeting.*
Iris: "You mean... because I invited you before...?"
Zoey: "Don't misunderstand. I just feel like a smoothie."
Zoey: "Will you take me or not?"
*I stare at Zoey blankly.*
*While her words are as cold as ever, I dare to think that she might be inviting me out. In her own way, that is.*
Iris: "O... Okay..."
Iris: "I mean, yeah. I'll definitely take you!"
Zoey: "Great."
*Zoey turns around and walks towards our home, which is in the same direction as the smoothie and ice cream store I had in mind.*
Iris: "H-Hey, wait! If you know where it is, why do you need an escort?"
*Zoey says nothing and continues walking.*
*As soon as we reach the ice cream store, we place our orders.*
*After my indecisiveness on whether I wanted an ice cream or a smoothie, Zoey insisted we order both.*
*Thanks to the ease involved in serving ice cream and blending smoothies, our orders are ready shortly after we arrive.*
Iris: "Uwah, that looks good!"
*I indicate Zoey's ice cream in a paper cup, which looks delicious.*
Iris: "I'll have to try the chocolate next time."
Zoey: "Mm, it is. Nothing beats chocolate ice cream."
Iris: "Eh~? I didn't pick you for a sweet tooth."
Zoey: "There is no human being alive who does not like chocolate."
Iris: "Hahaha, I can't argue with that."
*I take a bite of my strawberry ice cream, then wash it down with my shake. Mixed berries flavor.*
Zoey: "What about you? Do you like fruity flavors that much?"
Iris: "You could say that."
Iris: "Honestly, I love sweets of all kinds. Berry, chocolate, banana, it's all great."
Zoey: "Hmm... Well, you do look the type."
Iris: "Yeah, I-- Wait, what's that supposed to mean!?"
Zoey: "Nothing..."
*Zoey casually takes a sip of her chocolate smoothie.*
Iris: "Hmph! Fine then. If that's how you're going to be, I won't let you try any of mine."
Zoey: "Then it's a good thing I don't want any."
*True to her words, Zoey shows no interest in trying out my order. Or sharing any of hers, for that matter.*
Zoey: "Still... if you tell me I can't have any, that kind of makes me want some."
*Without warning Zoey reaches across and scoops up a spoonful of my ice cream.*
Iris: "Hey! I just said you *can't* have any!"
Zoey: "Hahaha..."
*Zoey reaches across once more, this time taking a sip of my smoothie.*
Zoey: "Mm. Not bad."
Iris: "Thief! Not only my ice cream, but my smoothie too!?"
Iris: "Fine then. If that's how it's going to be..."
*I grab Zoey's smoothie and bring it to my mouth.*
*Zoey doesn't stop me. Even with her smoothie an inch away from my lips, she doesn't move a muscle.*
Iris: "Um..."
Zoey: "What's wrong? Aren't you going to make us even?"
Iris: "That... was my intention."
Zoey: "And?"
*I look at the smoothie in my hands.*
*It dawns on me that Zoey has been drinking from this. If I went through with my plan, we would be sharing an indirect kiss.*
*Zoey already drank from my smoothie and used her own spoon, which had been in her mouth, to eat some of my ice cream. Obviously the thought didn't bother her.*
*Wait... of course it didn't. We're sisters, it's not like this is weird, or anything...*
*I hesitantly take a sip.*
Zoey: "Do you like it?"
Iris: "Y-Yeah... It tastes, uhh... chocolatey."
Zoey: "Duh."
*Zoey takes her smoothie back and immediately drinks from it.*
*I'm definitely thinking about this too much.*
Zoey: "Is this the first time you've been to an ice cream parlor with someone before?"
Iris: "Eh!?"
Iris: "O-Oh, uhm... Come to think of it, yeah. It is."
Iris: "Dad was busy with his job, and usually didn't have time for stuff like this."
Iris: "I spent most of my free time in after school lessons, or extra-curricular activities."
Iris: "My friends were bitter that I never went out with them, but I'd say I made the right choice. School comes first, right?"
*For the first time since we met up after school, Zoey's expression changes notably.*
*She looks surprised to hear what I have to say. Perhaps even shocked.*
Zoey: "So that's... how it was."
Iris: "Mm. I love Dad and all, but it was pretty lonely. You know what I mean?"
Iris: "...Ah, what am I saying? Your position is probably worse."
Iris: "In fact, now that you've left behind everyone you know and care about overseas, you're the one who should be complaining, not me."
Zoey: "N-No, that's... It's not that bad."
*Zoey looks sad for a moment. Without either one of us realizing it, the topic has grown rather heavy.*
*Back to her usual, serious expression, Zoey's words are the only indicator of her sadness.*
*Like me, she has been thrown around due to the circumstances of our parents.*
*But in her case, Zoey has come to this foreign school without her mother; hardly a parallel to my own situation.*
*I smile and take a scoop of Zoey's ice cream.*
Iris: "Apology accepted."
Zoey: "Thank you, Iris."
Iris: "Why don't we just put all of this behind us and start over. What do you say?"
Zoey: "Yeah... I'd like that."
*Zoey returns my words with a soft smile. It matches her cool, yet beautiful, face perfectly.*
*And for the first time since we were reunited, I feel as though I might be able to become friends with her after all.*

Iris: "Zoey! That was absolutely amazing!"
Iris: "No wonder you wanted to play so badly. I couldn't believe my ears!"
Zoey: "I-Iris, shh! Keep it down."
Zoey: "I'm supposed to be you, remember? Stop calling me Zoey."
Iris: "Oh... Right."
Iris: "Iris, that was absolutely--"
Zoey: "I get it, I get it. Stop already."
Zoey: "Besides, it was nothing special. I was just playing what they told me to."
Iris: "That's not true. I don't think you understand just how talented you are."
Iris: "Zoe-- Erm, Iris, I know I was skeptical about you playing in the evening, but I can see now how serious you are about this."
Iris: "I promise that I will be nothing but supportive from now on."
*Zoey scratches her face and averts her gaze.*
*She's blushing, as she has been since I first started to praise her.*

Zoey: "Anyway, you should head on home. I can't guarantee we'll finish on time, and it'll be getting dark by the time I'm done."

Iris: "I don't like it, but I'll do as you say. I have faith that you'll be alright."
Zoey: "Of course I will be. I'm not some helpless little girl, you know?"

Zoey: "You just hurry on home."

Iris: "Show them what my amazing little sister is made of!"
Zoey: "Uh... Y-Yeah. Thanks."

*Her figure retreats behind the curtain that separates the audience from the preparation area backstage, and I am alone once more.*
*Even so, I don't feel lonely or disheartened. In fact, I feel more excited than I did when I first heard that Zoey would be playing.*
*That's because I know that Zoey wants me here, listening to her play. She wants me to ease her nerves, and to give her the courage to play.*
*Behind the wall she puts up around herself, Zoey is starting to think of me as her sister. Someone whom she can rely on, and with whom she can share these special moments.*
*After being estranged for so many years, we are finally on our way to becoming a real family.*

*Though you can still see them in the city, it’s nothing compared to this.*

**click**
Iris: "Dad?"

*I can’t hear my father's breathing, or any other indicator of his mood.*
*The tension is so thick is starting to become cloying, and I can’t bring myself to say anything, either.*

*At this point, I want to hang up like I’d done before.*

Dad: "Zoey."
*He says her name not as a question, but as a statement of fact.*

Dad: "Your sister, Zoey."
Iris: "......"
Dad: "I..."
Dad: "I see."
Iris: "You see?"
Dad: "I heard what you said about your sister, yes."
*I wait, paralyzed, for what my father will say next.*
*But nothing comes.*
*After what feels like several minutes of silence, I get the courage to speak.*
Iris: "Dad?"
Dad: "I don’t know what to say, Iris."
Iris: "......"
Dad: "I’d always suspected you two were... but I’d hoped..."
Iris: "Hoped?"
*A loud sigh is his response.*
*I can picture my father alone in his office, with his face buried in his hand in shame from his daughters incestuous relationship.*
*At this mental image of my despondent father, the tears I’d been holding back break loose in hysterical sobs.*
Iris: "I’m sorry... I’m so sorry..."
Iris: "I didn’t mean to fall in love with her..."
Iris: "I... didn’t mean to..."

*My Dad sounds so disappointed, I-*
Dad: "Iris..."
Dad: "Calm down."

Dad: "Listen..."
*He’s quiet again, collecting himself for what he’s about to say next.*
Dad: "Iris, the only thing I’ve wanted for you is for you to be happy."
Dad: "I can’t say I understand this, because I don’t. Out of all the people on this earth who could give you a happy life with no hardships..."

Dad: "But... if Zoey is truly the one who makes you happy..."
Dad: "If she’s truly the one you love, then... I won’t get in your way."

Dad: "I’ve got to go now, honey. Hugs and kisses."
Iris: "Hugs and kisses..."
*With a click from the other line, our call ends.*
*Tears freely fall from my eyes. My father had told me all I wanted to hear. *
*I shakily lower the phone.*
*Dad hadn’t accepted my relationship with Zoey, but he hadn’t shunned it, either.*
*He hadn’t cast me out, hadn’t yelled at me, and hadn’t despised me for having a taboo relationship with his other daughter.*
*And that was more than enough.*
Zoey: "Hey."
Iris: "Zoey... Did you hear?"
Zoey: "A bit."
Zoey: "Do you think he... was alright with it?"
Iris: "Honestly? I don’t know."
Zoey: "......"

Iris: "But it wasn't for the reasons that I thought."

Zoey: "Because I love you, and I know he does, too."

Iris: "Yeah... you are."
Iris: "Alright, say "Yay camping!""

*Even though we hide our love from others, even though there are precious few places we could be a couple...*
*We still love each other more than ever.*
*So we’d face our future and everything in it...*
